<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Hurayra (companion); Zayd ibn Thabit (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z-Zinad from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman from Abu Hurayra and Zayd ibn Thabit that they saw no harm in eating what was cast up by the sea</p>

## Narration 1052

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1061)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 25</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنْ أَبِي سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، أَنَّ نَاسًا، مِنْ أَهْلِ الْجَارِ قَدِمُوا فَسَأَلُوا مَرْوَانَ بْنَ الْحَكَمِ عَمَّا لَفَظَ الْبَحْرُ فَقَالَ لَيْسَ بِهِ بَأْسٌ وَقَالَ اذْهَبُوا إِلَى زَيْدِ بْنِ ثَابِتٍ وَأَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ فَاسْأَلُوهُمَا عَنْ ذَلِكَ ثُمَّ ائْتُونِي فَأَخْبِرُونِي مَاذَا يَقُولاَنِ فَأَتَوْهُمَا فَسَأَلُوهُمَا فَقَالاَ لاَ بَأْسَ بِهِ ‏.‏ فَأَتَوْا مَرْوَانَ فَأَخْبَرُوهُ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ مَرْوَانُ قَدْ قُلْتُ لَكُمْ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ لاَ بَأْسَ بِأَكْلِ الْحِيتَانِ يَصِيدُهَا الْمَجُوسِيُّ لأَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ فِي الْبَحْرِ ‏ &quot;‏ هُوَ الطَّهُورُ مَاؤُهُ الْحِلُّ مَيْتَتُهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَإِذَا أُكِلَ ذَلِكَ مَيْتًا فَلاَ يَضُرُّهُ مَنْ صَادَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1061]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1061" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abu&#x27;z-Zinad from Abu Salama ibn Abd ar-Rahman that some people from al-Jar came to Marwan ibn al- Hakam and asked him about eating what was cast up by the sea. He said, &quot;There is no harm in eating it.&quot; Marwan said, &quot;Go to Zayd ibn Thabit and Abu Hurayra and ask them about it, then come to me and tell me what they say.&quot; They went to them and asked them, and they both said, &quot;There is no harm in eating it &quot; They returned to Marwan and told him. Marwan said, &quot;I told you.&quot; Malik said that there was no harm in eating fish caught by magians, because the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;In the sea&#x27;s water is purity, and that which is dead in it is halal. &quot; Malik said, &quot;If it is eaten when it is dead, there is no harm in who catches it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1061]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1061" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबी सलमा बिन अब्दुर्रहमान (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) से रिवायत है के कुछ लोग ज़र के रहने वाले मरवान के पास आए और पूछा के जिस जानवर को दरिया फेंक दे उस का क्या हुकुम है? मरवान ने कहा इस का खाना दुरुस्त है और तुम जाओ ज़ैद बिन साबित और अबू हुरैरा के पास और पूछो उन से, फिर मुझ को आ कर ख़बर करो क्या कहते हैं। उन्हों ने पूछा इन दोनों से, दोनों ने कहा दुरुस्त है, उन लोगों ने फिर आ कर मरवान से कहा, मरवान ने कहा मैं तो तुम से पहले ही कह चुका था।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1061]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1061" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابی سلمہ بن عبدالرحمن رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ روایت ہے کہ کچھ لوگ جار کے رہنے والے مروان کے پاس آئے اور پوچھا کہ جس جانور کو دریا پیھنک دے اس کا کیا حکم ہے مروان نے کہا اس کا کھانا درست ہے اور تم جاؤ زید بن ثابت اور ابوہریرہ کے پاس اور پوچھو ان سے پھر مجھ کو آن کر خبر کرو کیا کہتے ہیں، انہوں نے پوچھا ان دونوں سے دونوں نے کہا درست ہے ان لوگوں نے پھر آن کر مروان سے کہا مروان نے کہا میں نے تو تم سے پہلے ہی کہہ چکا تھا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1061]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1061" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abi Salama bin Abdurrahman (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke kuch log Jar ke rehne wale Marwan ke paas aaye aur poocha ke jis janwar ko darya phenk de us ka kya hukum hai? Marwan ne kaha is ka khana durust hai aur tum jao Zaid bin Thabit aur Abu Huraira ke paas aur poocho un se, phir mujh ko aa kar khabar karo kya kehte hain. Unhon ne poocha in dono se, dono ne kaha durust hai, un logon ne phir aa kar Marwan se kaha, Marwan ne kaha main to tum se pehle hi keh chuka tha.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1061]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1061" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1053

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1062)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 25</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنِ أَبِي إِدْرِيسَ الْخَوْلاَنِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي ثَعْلَبَةَ الْخُشَنِيِّ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَكْلُ كُلِّ ذِي نَابٍ مِنَ السِّبَاعِ حَرَامٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1062]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1062" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

## Narration 1054

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1063)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 25</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ إِسْمَاعِيلَ بْنِ أَبِي حَكِيمٍ، عَنْ عَبِيدَةَ بْنِ سُفْيَانَ الْحَضْرَمِيِّ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ أَكْلُ كُلِّ ذِي نَابٍ مِنَ السِّبَاعِ حَرَامٌ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1063]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1063" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ismail ibn Abi Hakim from Abiyda ibn Sufyan al-Hadrami from Abu Hurayra that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &quot;Eating animals with fangs is haram. &quot; Malik said, &quot;This is the custom among us</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1063]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1063" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1055

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1064)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 25</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُبَيْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُتْبَةَ بْنِ مَسْعُودٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ مَرَّ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِشَاةٍ مَيِّتَةٍ كَانَ أَعْطَاهَا مَوْلاَةً لِمَيْمُونَةَ زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ أَفَلاَ انْتَفَعْتُمْ بِجِلْدِهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ فَقَالُوا يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ إِنَّهَا مَيْتَةٌ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏&quot;‏ إِنَّمَا حُرِّمَ أَكْلُهَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1064]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1064" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1064</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1064 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that the best of what he had heard about horses, mules, and donkeys was that they were not eaten because Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted,said, &quot;And horses, and mules and asses, for you to ride, and as an adornment. &quot; (Sura 16 ayat 8) . He said, may He be Blessed and Exalted, &quot;In cattle, some of them you ride, and some of them you eat.&quot; (Sura 6 ayat 79). He said, the Blessed, the Exalted, &quot;Mention Allah&#x27;s name over what He has provided you of cattle, and eat of them and feed the beggar (al-qani) and the suppliant (al-mutarr). (Sura 22 ayat 34). Malik said &quot;Allah mentioned horses, mules, and donkeys for riding and adornment, and He mentioned cattle for riding and eating.&quot; Malik said, &quot;Al-qani also means the poor.&quot; Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Ubaydullah ibn Abdullah ibn Utba ibn Masud that Abdullah ibn Abbas said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, passed by a dead sheep which had been given to a mawla of his wife, Maimuna. He said, &#x27; Aren&#x27;t you going to use its skin?&#x27; They said, &#x27;Messenger of Allah, but it is carrion. &#x27;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said, &#x27;Only eating it is haram</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1064]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1064" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हु) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) अपनी ज़ौजा मैमूना के ग़ुलाम की मुर्दार बकरी के पास से गुज़रे, यह बकरी आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने इस ग़ुलाम को दी थी। फ़रमाया आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने क्यों काम में न लाए तुम खाल इस की, उन्हों ने कहा या रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) वो मुर्दार है, आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने कहा मुर्दार का खाना हराम है।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1064]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1064" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبداللہ بن عباس رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہما سے روایت ہے کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم اپنی زوجہ میمونہ کے غلام کی مردار بکری کے پاس سے گزرے ، یہ بکری آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے اس غلام کو دی تھی۔ فرمایا آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے کیوں کام میں نہ لائے تم کھال اس کی، انہوں نے کہا یا رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم وہ مردار ہے آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے کہا مردار کا کھانا حرام ہے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1064]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1064" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat hai ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) apni zauja Maimoona ke ghulam ki murdar bakri ke paas se guzre, yeh bakri Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne is ghulam ko di thi. Farmaya Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne kyun kaam mein na laye tum khaal is ki, unhon ne kaha Ya Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) woh murdar hai, Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne kaha murdar ka khana haram hai.</p>

## Narration 1056

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1065)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 25</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي مَالِكٌ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنِ ابْنِ وَعْلَةَ الْمِصْرِيِّ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عَبَّاسٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا دُبِغَ الإِهَابُ فَقَدْ طَهُرَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1065]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1065" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

## Narration 1057

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1066)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 25</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَزِيدَ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ قُسَيْطٍ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ ثَوْبَانَ، عَنْ أُمِّهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، زَوْجِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَمَرَ أَنْ يُسْتَمْتَعَ بِجُلُودِ الْمَيْتَةِ إِذَا دُبِغَتْ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1066]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1066" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1066</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1066 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yazid ibn Abdullah ibn Qusayt from Muhammad ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn Thawban from his mother that A&#x27;isha, the wife of the Prophet, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, ordered that the skins of carrion be used after they had been tanned</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1066]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1066" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रात आयेशा ज़ौजा नबी (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से रिवायत है के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया मुर्दार की खालों से नफा उठाने को जब डबघात कर ली जाएँ.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1066]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">

<p dir="rtl">حضرت عائشہ زوجہ نبی صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے روایت ہے کہ کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا مردار کی کھالوں سے نفع اٹھانے کو جب دباغت کرلی جائیں ۔</p>

## Narration 1058

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1067)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 26</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، عَنْ رَجُلٍ، مِنْ بَنِي ضَمْرَةَ عَنْ أَبِيهِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ سُئِلَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنِ الْعَقِيقَةِ فَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ لاَ أُحِبُّ الْعُقُوقَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَكَأَنَّهُ إِنَّمَا كَرِهَ الاِسْمَ وَقَالَ ‏&quot;‏ مَنْ وُلِدَ لَهُ وَلَدٌ فَأَحَبَّ أَنْ يَنْسُكَ عَنْ وَلَدِهِ فَلْيَفْعَلْ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1067]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1067" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1067</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1067 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that the best of what he had heard about a man who is forced by necessity to eat carrion is that he ate it until he was full and then he took provision from it. If he found something which would enable him to dispense with it, he threw it away. Malik when asked whether or not a man who had been forced by necessity to eat carrion, should eat it when he also found the fruit, crops or sheep of a people in that place, answered, &quot;If he thinks that the owners of the fruit, crops, or sheep will believe his necessity so that he will not be deemed a thief and have his hand cut off, then I think that he should eat from whatever he finds that which will remove his hunger but he should not carry any of it away. I prefer that he does that than that he eat carrion. If he fears that he will not be believed, and will be deemed a thief for what he has taken, then I think that it is better for him to eat the carrion, and he has leeway to eat carrion in this respect. Even so, I fear that someone who is not forced by necessity to eat carrion might exceed the limits out of a desire to consume other peoples&#x27; property, crops or fruit.&quot; Malik said, &quot;That is the best of what I have heard.&quot; Yahya related to me from Zayd ibn Aslam from a man of the Banu Damra that his father said, &quot;The Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, was asked about the Aqiqa. He said, &#x27;I do not like disobedience (uquq),&#x27; as if he disliked the name. He said, &#x27;If anyone has a child born to him, and wants to sacrifice for his child, then let him do it</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1067]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1067" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">बानी ज़मरा के एक शख्स से रिवायत है उस ने अपने बाप से सुना के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से अक़ीक़े के बारे में पुछा गया तो आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया मैं &#x27;उक़ूक़&#x27; को पसंद नहीं करता यानि इस नाम को नापसंद किया और फ़रमाया जिस शख्स का बच्चा पैदा हो और वह अपने बच्चे की तरफ से क़ुरबानी करना चाहे तो करे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1067]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1067" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">بنی ضمرہ کے ایک شخص سے روایت ہے اس نے اپنے باپ سے سنا کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم سے عقیقے کے بارے پوچھا گیا تو آپ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا میں عقوق کو پسند نہیں کرتا یعنی اس نام کو ناپسند کیا اور فرمایا جس شخص کا بچہ پیدا ہو اور وہ اپنے بچے کی طرف سے قربانی کرنا چاہے تو کرے ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1067]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1067" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Bani Zamra ke ek shakhs se riwayat hai us ne apne baap se suna ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se aqiqe ke bare mein poocha gaya to Aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya main &#x27;Uqooq&#x27; ko pasand nahi karta yani is naam ko napasand kiya aur farmaya jis shakhs ka bacha paida ho aur woh apne bache ki taraf se qurbani karna chahe to kare.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1067]</p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Fatima (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Jafar ibn Muhammad that his father said, &quot;Fatima, the daughter of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, weighed the hair of Hasan, Husayn, Zaynab and Umm Kulthum, and gave away in sadaqa an equivalent weight of silver</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1068]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ عَلِيِّ بْنِ الْحُسَيْنِ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ وَزَنَتْ فَاطِمَةُ بِنْتُ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم شَعَرَ حَسَنٍ وَحُسَيْنٍ فَتَصَدَّقَتْ بِزِنَتِهِ فِضَّةً ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1069" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Fatima (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Rabia ibn Abi Abd ar-Rahman that Muhammad ibn Ali ibn al-Husayn said, &quot;Fatima, the daughter of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, weighed the hair of Hasan and Husayn, and gave away in sadaqa the equivalent weight in silver</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1069]</p>

<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Nafi that if any of Abdullah ibn Umar&#x27;s family asked him for an aqiqa, he would give it to them. He gave a sheep as aqiqa for both his male and female children</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1070]</p>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ رَبِيعَةَ بْنِ أَبِي عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ، عَنْ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ إِبْرَاهِيمَ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ التَّيْمِيِّ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ سَمِعْتُ أَبِي يَسْتَحِبُّ الْعَقِيقَةَ، وَلَوْ بِعُصْفُورٍ ‏.‏</p>

## Narration 1064

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 26</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1073]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1073" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1073</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1073 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، أَنَّ أَبَاهُ، عُرْوَةَ بْنَ الزُّبَيْرِ كَانَ يَعُقُّ عَنْ بَنِيهِ الذُّكُورِ، وَالإِنَاثِ، بِشَاةٍ شَاةٍ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ عِنْدَنَا فِي الْعَقِيقَةِ أَنَّ مَنْ عَقَّ فَإِنَّمَا يَعُقُّ عَنْ وَلَدِهِ بِشَاةٍ شَاةٍ الذُّكُورِ وَالإِنَاثِ وَلَيْسَتِ الْعَقِيقَةُ بِوَاجِبَةٍ وَلَكِنَّهَا يُسْتَحَبُّ الْعَمَلُ بِهَا وَهِيَ مِنَ الأَمْرِ الَّذِي لَمْ يَزَلْ عَلَيْهِ النَّاسُ عِنْدَنَا فَمَنْ عَقَّ عَنْ وَلَدِهِ فَإِنَّمَا هِيَ بِمَنْزِلَةِ النُّسُكِ وَالضَّحَايَا لاَ يَجُوزُ فِيهَا عَوْرَاءُ وَلاَ عَجْفَاءُ وَلاَ مَكْسُورَةٌ وَلاَ مَرِيضَةٌ وَلاَ يُبَاعُ مِنْ لَحْمِهَا شَىْءٌ وَلاَ جِلْدُهَا وَيُكْسَرُ عِظَامُهَا وَيَأْكُلُ أَهْلُهَا مِنْ لَحْمِهَا وَيَتَصَدَّقُونَ مِنْهَا وَلاَ يُمَسُّ الصَّبِيُّ بِشَىْءٍ مِنْ دَمِهَا ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: successor report. Attribution: Urwa ibn az-Zubayr (successor). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Hisham ibn Urwa that his father, Urwa ibn az-Zubayr made an aqiqa for his male and female children of a sheep each.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1073]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1073" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1073</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1073 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;What we do about the aqiqa is that if someone makes an aqiqa for his children, he gives a sheep for both male and female. The aqiqa is not obligatory but it is desirable to do it, and people continue to come to us about it. If someone makes an aqiqa for his children, the same rules apply as with all sacrificial animals - one-eyed, emaciated, injured, or sick animals must not be used, and neither the meat or the skin is to be sold. The bones are broken and the family eat the meat and give some of it away as sadaqa. The child is not smeared with any of the blood</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1073" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1065

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1074)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 27</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1074]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1074" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1074</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1074 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ أَنَّ مُعَاوِيَةَ بْنَ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ، كَتَبَ إِلَى زَيْدِ بْنِ ثَابِتٍ يَسْأَلُهُ عَنِ الْجَدِّ، فَكَتَبَ إِلَيْهِ زَيْدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ إِنَّكَ كَتَبْتَ إِلَىَّ تَسْأَلُنِي عَنِ الْجَدِّ، وَاللَّهُ، أَعْلَمُ وَذَلِكَ مِمَّا لَمْ يَكُنْ يَقْضِي فِيهِ إِلاَّ الأُمَرَاءُ - يَعْنِي الْخُلَفَاءَ - وَقَدْ حَضَرْتُ الْخَلِيفَتَيْنِ قَبْلَكَ يُعْطِيَانِهِ النِّصْفَ مَعَ الأَخِ الْوَاحِدِ وَالثُّلُثَ مَعَ الاِثْنَيْنِ فَإِنْ كَثُرَتِ الإِخْوَةُ لَمْ يُنَقِّصُوهُ مِنَ الثُّلُثِ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1074" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">This reading preserves separate source spans. Intervening source notes are retained under Comments. Report type: companion report. Attribution: Muawiya ibn Abi Sufyan (companion); Zayd ibn Thabit (companion). The final correspondence report is selected in two exact passages around an editorial gloss. The extensive preceding inheritance exposition remains context; the passages are one source report. Some reports contain separate source passages. Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Source report 1</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that he had heard that Muawiya ibn Abi Sufyan wrote to Zayd ibn Thabit asking him about the grandfather. Zayd ibn Thabit wrote to him, &quot;You have written to me asking me about the grandfather. Allah knows best. That is part of what is only determined by the amirs</p>

<p class="counterpart-note">Intervening source text is retained under Comments.</p>

<p dir="ltr"> I was present with two khalifs before you who gave the grandfather a half with one sibling, and a third with two. If there were more siblings, they did not decrease his third</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1074]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1074" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1074</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1074 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik, &quot;The generally agreed upon way of doing things among us and what I have seen the people of knowledge doing in our city about the fixed shares of inheritance of children from the mother or father when one or other of them dies is that if they leave male and female children, the male takes the portion of two females. If there are only females, and there are more than two, they get two thirds of what is left between them. If there is only one, she gets a half. If someone shares with the children, who has a fixed share and there are males among them, the reckoner begins with the ones with fixed shares. What remains after that is divided among the children according to their inheritance. &quot;When there are no children, grandchildren through sons have the same position as children, so that grandsons are like sons and grand-daughters are like daughters. They inherit as they inherit and they overshadow as they overshadow. If there are both children and grandchildren through sons, and there is a male among the children, then the grandchildren through sons do not share in the inheritance with him. &quot;If there is no surviving male among the children, and there are two or more daughters, the granddaughters through a son do not share in the inheritance with them unless there is a male who is in the same position as them in relation to the deceased, or further than them. His presence gives access to whatever is left over, if any, to whoever is in his position and whoever is above him of the granddaughters through sons. If something is left over, they divide it among them, and the male takes the portion of two females. If nothing is left over, they have nothing. &quot;If the only descendant is a daughter, she takes half, and if there are one or more grand-daughters through a son who are in the same position to the deceased, they share a sixth. If there is a male in the same position as the granddaughters through a son in relation to the deceased, they have no share and no sixth . &quot;If there is a surplus after the allotting of shares to the people with fixed shares, the surplus goes to the male and whoever is in his position and whoever is above him of the female descendants through sons. The male has the share of two females. The one who is more distant in relationship than grandchildren through sons has nothing. If there is no surplus, they have nothing. That is because Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, said in His Book, &#x27;Allah charges you about your children that the male has the like of the portion of two females. If there are more than two women they have two thirds of what is left. If there is one, she has a half.&#x27; (Sura 4 ayat 10) Malik said, &quot;The inheritance of a husband from a wife when she leaves no children or grandchildren through sons is a half. If she leaves children or grandchildren through sons, male or female, by her present or previous husbands, the husband has a quarter after bequests or debts. The inheritance of a wife from a husband who does not leave children or grandchildren through sons is a quarter. If he leaves children or grandchildren through sons, male or female, the wife has an eighth after bequests and debts. That is because Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted! said in His Book, &#x27;You have a half of what your wives leave if they have no children. If they have children, you have a fourth of what they leave after bequests and debts. They have a fourth of what you leave if you have no children. If you have children, they have an eighth after bequests or debts.&#x27; &quot; (Sura4ayat 11). Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us about which there is no dispute and what I have seen the people of knowledge in our city doing is that when a father inherits from a son or a daughter and the deceased leaves children, or grandchildren through a son, the father has a fixed share of one sixth. If the deceased does leave any children or male grandchildren through a son, the apportioning begins with those with whom the father shares in the fixed shares. They are given their fixed shares. If a sixth or more is left over, the sixth and what is above it is given to the father, and if there is less than a sixth left, the father is given his sixth as a fixed share, (i.e. the other shares are adjusted.) &quot;The inheritance of a mother from her child, if her son or daughter dies and leaves children or male or female grandchildren through a son, or leaves two or more full or half siblings is a sixth. If the deceased does not leave any children or grandchildren through a son, or two or more siblings, the mother has a whole third except in two cases. One of them is if a man dies and leaves a wife and both parents. The wife has a fourth, the mother a third of what remains, (which is a fourth of the capital). The other is if a wife dies and leaves a husband and both parents. The husband gets half, and the mother a third of what remains, (which is a sixth of the capital). That is because Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, says in His Book, &#x27;His two parents each have a sixth of what he leaves if he has children. If he does not have children, and his parents inherit from him, his mother has a third. If he has siblings, the mother has a sixth.&#x27; (Sura 4 ayat 11). The sunna is that the siblings be two or more.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed upon way of doing things among us is that maternal half-siblings do not inherit anything when there are children or grandchildren through sons, male or female. They do not inherit anything when there is a father or the father&#x27;s father. They inherit in what is outside of that. If there is only one male or female, they are given a sixth. If there are two, each of them has a sixth. If there are more than that, they share in a third which is divided among them. The male does not have portion of two females. That is because Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, says in His Book, &#x27;If a man or woman has no direct heir, and he has a brother or sister, by the mother, each of them has a sixth. If there are more than two, they share equally in a third.&#x27; &quot; (Sura 4 ayat 12). Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us is that full siblings do not inherit anything with sons nor anything with grandsons through a son, nor anything with the father. They do inherit with the daughters and the granddaughters through a son when the deceased does not leave a paternal grandfather. Any property that is left over, they are in it as paternal relations. One begins with the people who are allotted fixed shares. They are given their shares. If there is anything left over after that, it belongs to the full siblings. They divide it between themselves according to the Book of Allah, whether they are male or female. The male has a portion of two females. If there is nothing left over, they have nothing. &quot;If the deceased does not leave a father or a paternal grandfather or children or male or female grandchildren through a son, a single full sister gets a half. If there are two or more full sisters, they get two thirds. If there is a brother with them, sisters, whether one or more, do not have a fixed share. One begins with whoever shares in the fixed shares. They are given their shares. Whatever remains after that goes to the full siblings. The male has the portion of two females except in one case, in which the full siblings have nothing. They share in this case the third of the half-siblings by the mother. That case is when a woman dies and leaves a husband, a mother, half- siblings by her mother, and full siblings. The husband has a half. The mother has one sixth. The half-siblings by the mother have a third. Nothing is left after that, so the full siblings share in this case with the half-siblings by the mother in their third. The male has the portion of two females in as much as all of them are siblings of the deceased by the mother. They inherit by the mother. That is because Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, said in His Book, &#x27;If a man or a woman has no direct heir and he has a brother or a sister, each one of the two gets a sixth. If there are more than that, they share equally in the third. &#x27; (Sura 4 ayat 12) . They therefore share in this case because all of them are siblings of the deceased by the mother.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on wayof doing things among us is that when there are no full siblings with them, half-siblings by the father take the position of full siblings. Their males are like the males of the full siblings, and their females are like their females except in the case where the half-siblings by the mother and the full siblings share, because they are not offspring of the mother who joins these.&quot; Malik said, &quot;If there are both full siblings and half-siblings by the father and there is a male among the full siblings none of the half-siblings by the father have any inheritance. If there is one or more females in the full siblings and there is no male with them, the one full sister gets a half, and the half sister by the father gets a sixth, completing the two-thirds. If there is a male with the half-sisters by the father, they have no share. The people of fixed shares are given their shares and if there is something left after that it is divided between the half-siblings by the father. The male has the portion of two females. If there is nothing left over, they get nothing. If the full siblings consist of two or more females, they get two-thirds, and the half-sisters by the father get nothing with them unless there is a half-brother by the father with them. If there is a half-brother by the father with them, the people of fixed shares are given their shares and if there is something left over after that, it is divided between the half- siblings by the father. The male gets the portion of two females. If there is nothing left over, they get nothing. Half-siblings by the mother, full-siblings, and half-siblings by the father, each have a sixth (when they are onlyone). Two and more share a third. The male has the same portion as the female. They are in the same position in it.&quot; </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1074" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr">, i.e. the khalifs.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1074" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">मुआवियाह बिन आबि सुफ़यान ने लिखा ज़ैद बिन थाबित को और पुछा दादा की मीरास के मुताल्लिक़. ज़ैद बिन थाबित ने जवाब लिखा कि तुमने मुझसे पुछा दादा की मीरास के मुताल्लिक़ और ये वह मसाला है जिसमे खुलफ़ा हुकुम करते थे. मैं हाज़िर था तुमसे पहले दो खुलफ़ा के सामने तो एक भाई के साथ वह दादा को निस्फ़ (आधा) दिलाते थे और दो भाइयों के साथ थुलथ (तीसरा हिस्सा) अगर बहुत भाई बहिन होते तब भी दादा को थुलथ से काम न दिलाते.</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">معاویہ بن ابی سفیان نے لکھا زید بن ثابت کو اور پوچھا دادا کی میراث کے متعلق زید بن ثابت نے جواب لکھا کہ تم نے مجھ سے پوچھا دادا کی میراث کے متعلق اور یہ وہ مسئلہ ہے جس میں خلفاء حکم کرتے تھے میں حاضر تھا تم سے پہلے دو خلیفاؤں کے سامنے تو ایک بھائی کے ساتھ وہ دادا کو نصف دلاتے تھے اور دو بھائیوں کے ساتھ ثلث اگر بہت بھائی بہن ہوتے تب بھی دادا کو ثلث سے کم نہ دلاتے ۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Muawiyah bin Abi Sufyan ne likha Zaid bin Thabit ko aur poocha dada ki meeras ke mutalliq. Zaid bin Thabit ne jawab likha ke tumne mujhse poocha dada ki meeras ke mutalliq aur ye woh masala hai jisme khulafa hukum karte the. Main hazir tha tumse pehle do khulafa ke samne to ek bhai ke sath woh dada ko nisf (aadha) dilate the aur do bhaiyon ke sath thulth (tisra hissa) agar bahut bhai behan hote tab bhi dada ko thulth se kam na dilate.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1074" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1067

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1076)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 27</strong></p>

<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1076]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1076" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّهُ بَلَغَهُ عَنْ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنِ يَسَارٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ فَرَضَ عُمَرُ بْنُ الْخَطَّابِ وَعُثْمَانُ بْنُ عَفَّانَ وَزَيْدُ بْنُ ثَابِتٍ لِلْجَدِّ مَعَ الإِخْوَةِ الثُّلُثَ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا وَالَّذِي أَدْرَكْتُ عَلَيْهِ أَهْلَ الْعِلْمِ بِبَلَدِنَا أَنَّ الْجَدَّ أَبَا الأَبِ لاَ يَرِثُ مَعَ الأَبِ دِنْيَا شَيْئًا وَهُوَ يُفْرَضُ لَهُ مَعَ الْوَلَدِ الذَّكَرِ وَمَعَ ابْنِ الاِبْنِ الذَّكَرِ السُّدُسُ فَرِيضَةً وَهُوَ فِيمَا سِوَى ذَلِكَ مَا لَمْ يَتْرُكِ الْمُتَوَفَّى أَخًا أَوْ أُخْتًا لأَبِيهِ يُبَدَّأُ بِأَحَدٍ إِنْ شَرَّكَهُ بِفَرِيضَةٍ مُسَمَّاةٍ فَيُعْطَوْنَ فَرَائِضَهُمْ فَإِنْ فَضَلَ مِنَ الْمَالِ السُّدُسُ فَمَا فَوْقَهُ فُرِضَ لِلْجَدِّ السُّدُسُ فَرِيضَةً ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَالْجَدُّ وَالإِخْوَةُ لِلأَبِ وَالأُمِّ إِذَا شَرَّكَهُمْ أَحَدٌ بِفَرِيضَةٍ مُسَمَّاةٍ يُبَدَّأُ بِمَنْ شَرَّكَهُمْ مِنْ أَهْلِ الْفَرَائِضِ فَيُعْطَوْنَ فَرَائِضَهُمْ فَمَا بَقِيَ بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ لِلْجَدِّ وَالإِخْوَةِ مِنْ شَىْءٍ فَإِنَّهُ يُنْظَرُ أَىُّ ذَلِكَ أَفْضَلُ لِحَظِّ الْجَدِّ أُعْطِيَهُ الثُّلُثُ مِمَّا بَقِيَ لَهُ وَلِلإِخْوَةِ أَوْ يَكُونُ بِمَنْزِلَةِ رَجُلٍ مِنَ الإِخْوَةِ فِيمَا يَحْصُلُ لَهُ وَلَهُمْ يُقَاسِمُهُمْ بِمِثْلِ حِصَّةِ أَحَدِهِمْ أَوِ السُّدُسُ مِنْ رَأْسِ الْمَالِ كُلِّهِ أَىُّ ذَلِكَ كَانَ أَفْضَلَ لِحَظِّ الْجَدِّ أُعْطِيَهُ الْجَدُّ وَكَانَ مَا بَقِيَ بَعْدَ ذَلِكَ لِلإِخْوَةِ لِلأَبِ وَالأُمِّ لِلذَّكَرِ مِثْلُ حَظِّ الأُنْثَيَيْنِ إِلاَّ فِي فَرِيضَةٍ وَاحِدَةٍ تَكُونُ قِسْمَتُهُمْ فِيهَا عَلَى غَيْرِ ذَلِكَ وَتِلْكَ الْفَرِيضَةُ امْرَأَةٌ تُوُفِّيَتْ وَتَرَكَتْ زَوْجَهَا وَأُمَّهَا وَأُخْتَهَا لأُمِّهَا وَأَبِيهَا وَجَدَّهَا فَلِلزَّوْجِ النِّصْفُ وَلِلأُمِّ الثُّلُثُ وَلِلْجَدِّ السُّدُسُ وَلِلأُخْتِ لِلأُمِّ وَالأَبِ النِّصْفُ ثُمَّ يُجْمَعُ سُدُسُ الْجَدِّ وَنِصْفُ الأُخْتِ فَيُقْسَمُ أَثْلاَثًا لِلذَّكَرِ مِثْلُ حَظِّ الأُنْثَيَيْنِ فَيَكُونُ لِلْجَدِّ ثُلُثَاهُ وَلِلأُخْتِ ثُلُثُهُ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَمِيرَاثُ الإِخْوَةِ لِلأَبِ مَعَ الْجَدِّ إِذَا لَمْ يَكُنْ مَعَهُمْ إِخْوَةٌ لأَبٍ وَأُمٍّ كَمِيرَاثِ الإِخْوَةِ لِلأَبِ وَالأُمِّ سَوَاءٌ ذَكَرُهُمْ كَذَكَرِهِمْ وَأُنْثَاهُمْ كَأُنْثَاهُمْ فَإِذَا اجْتَمَعَ الإِخْوَةُ لِلأَبِ وَالأُمِّ وَالإِخْوَةُ لِلأَبِ فَإِنَّ الإِخْوَةَ لِلأَبِ وَالأُمِّ يُعَادُّونَ الْجَدَّ بِإِخْوَتِهِمْ لأَبِيهِمْ فَيَمْنَعُونَهُ بِهِمْ كَثْرَةَ الْمِيرَاثِ بِعَدَدِهِمْ وَلاَ يُعَادُّونَهُ بِالإِخْوَةِ لِلأُمِّ لأَنَّهُ لَوْ لَمْ يَكُنْ مَعَ الْجَدِّ غَيْرُهُمْ لَمْ يَرِثُوا مَعَهُ شَيْئًا وَكَانَ الْمَالُ كُلُّهُ لِلْجَدِّ فَمَا حَصَلَ لِلإِخْوَةِ مِنْ بَعْدِ حَظِّ الْجَدِّ فَإِنَّهُ يَكُونُ لِلإِخْوَةِ مِنَ الأَبِ وَالأُمِّ دُونَ الإِخْوَةِ لِلأَبِ وَلاَ يَكُونُ لِلإِخْوَةِ لِلأَبِ مَعَهُمْ شَىْءٌ إِلاَّ أَنْ يَكُونَ الإِخْوَةُ لِلأَبِ وَالأُمِّ امْرَأَةً وَاحِدَةً فَإِنْ كَانَتِ امْرَأَةً وَاحِدَةً فَإِنَّهَا تُعَادُّ الْجَدَّ بِإِخْوَتِهَا لأَبِيهَا مَا كَانُوا فَمَا حَصَلَ لَهُمْ وَلَهَا مِنْ شَىْءٍ كَانَ لَهَا دُونَهُمْ مَا بَيْنَهَا وَبَيْنَ أَنْ تَسْتَكْمِلَ فَرِيضَتَهَا وَفَرِيضَتُهَا النِّصْفُ مِنْ رَأْسِ الْمَالِ كُلِّهِ فَإِنْ كَانَ فِيمَا يُحَازُ لَهَا وَلإِخْوَتِهَا لأَبِيهَا فَضْلٌ عَنْ نِصْفِ رَأْسِ الْمَالِ كُلِّهِ فَهُوَ لإِخْوَتِهَا لأَبِيهَا لِلذَّكَرِ مِثْلُ حَظِّ الأُنْثَيَيْنِ فَإِنْ لَمْ يَفْضُلْ شَىْءٌ فَلاَ شَىْءَ لَهُمْ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Umar ibn al-Khattab (companion); Uthman ibn Affan (companion); Zayd ibn Thabit (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik that he had heard that Sulayman ibn Yasar said, &#x27;&#x27;Umar ibn al-Khattab, Uthman ibn Affan, andZayd ibn Thabit gave the grandfather a third with full siblings&quot;.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1076]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1076" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1076</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1076 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr"> Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us and what I have seen the people of knowledge in our city doing is that the paternal grandfather does not inherit anything at all with the father. He is given a sixth as a fixed share with the son and the grandson through a son. Other than that, when the deceased does not leave a mother or a paternal aunt, one begins with whoever has a fixed share, and they are given their shares. If there is a sixth of the property left over, the grandfather is given a sixth as a fixed share.&quot; Malik said, &quot;When someone shares with the grandfather and the full siblings in a specified share, one begins with whoever shares with them of the people of fixed shares. They are given their shares. What is left over after that belongs to the grandfather and the full siblings. Then one sees which is the more favourable of two alternatives for the portion of the grandfather. Either a third is allotted to him and the siblings to divide between them, and he gets a share as if he were one of the siblings, or else he takes a sixth from all the capital. Whichever is the best portion for the grandfather is given to him. What is left after that, goes to the full siblings. The male gets the portion of two females except in one particular case. The division in this case is different from the preceding one. This case is when a woman dies and leaves a husband, mother, full sister and grandfather. The husband gets a half, the mother gets a third, the grandfather gets a sixth, and the full sister gets a half. The sixth of the grandfather and the half of the sister are joined and divided into thirds. The male gets the share of two females. Therefore, the grandfather has two thirds, and the sister has one third.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The inheritance of the half-siblings by the father with the grandfather when there are no full siblings with them, is like the inheritance of the full siblings (in the same situation). The males are the same as their males and the females are the same as their females. When there are both full siblings and half-siblings by the father, the full siblings include in their number the number of half-siblings by the father, to limit the inheritance of the grandfather, i.e., if there was only one full sibling with the grandfather. They would share, after the allotting of the fixed shares, the remainder of the inheritance between them equally. If there were also two half-siblings by the father, their number is added to the division of the sum, which would then be divided four ways. A quarter going to the grandfather and three-quarters going to the full siblings who annex the shares technically allotted to the half-siblings by the father. They do not include the number of half-siblings by the mother, because if there were only half-siblings by the father they would not inherit anything with the grandfather and all the capital would belong to the grandfather, and so the siblings would not get anything after the portion of the grandfather. &quot;It belongs to the full siblings more than the half-siblings by the father, and the half-siblings by the father do not get anything with them unless the full siblings consist of one sister. If there is one full sister, she includes the grandfather with the half-siblings by her father in the division, however many. Whatever remains for her and these half-siblings by the father goes to her rather than them until she has had her complete share, which is half of the total capital. If there is surplus beyond half of all the capital in what she and the half-siblings by the father acquire it goes to them. The male has the portion of two females. If there is nothing left over, they get nothing</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1076" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से रिवायत की के उन्होंने सुना के सुलैमान बिन यसार ने कहा: उमर बिन अल-खत्ताब, उस्मान बिन अफ़्फ़ान, और ज़ैद बिन साबित (रदी अल्लाहू अन्हुम) ने दादा को सगे बहन भाइयों के साथ एक तिहाई हिस्सा दिया। इमाम मालिक ने कहा: हमारे हाँ जो तरीक़ा मुत्तफ़िक़ा है और जो मैं ने हमारे शहर के अहल-ए-इल्म को करते देखा है वो यह है के दादा बाप की मौजूदगी में बिल्कुल वारिस नहीं होता। उसे बेटे और पोते के साथ छठा हिस्सा बतौरे-फ़र्ज़ दिया जाता है। इस के इलावा, जब मय्यत की माँ या फूफी न हो, तो पहले असहाब-ए-फ़र्ज़ को उन के हिस्से दिए जाते हैं। अगर छठा हिस्सा बच जाए तो वो दादा को मिल जाता है।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1076" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک سے روایت کی کہ انہوں نے سنا کہ سلیمان بن یسار نے کہا: عمر بن خطاب، عثمان بن عفان اور زید بن ثابت (رضی اللہ عنہم) نے دادا کو سگے بہن بھائیوں کے ساتھ ایک تہائی حصہ دیا۔ امام مالک نے کہا: ہمارے ہاں جو طریقہ متفقہ ہے اور جو میں نے ہمارے شہر کے اہل علم کو کرتے دیکھا ہے وہ یہ ہے کہ دادا باپ کی موجودگی میں بالکل وارث نہیں ہوتا۔ اسے بیٹے اور پوتے کے ساتھ چھٹا حصہ بطور فرض دیا جاتا ہے۔ اس کے علاوہ، جب میت کی ماں یا پھوپھی نہ ہو، تو پہلے اصحابِ فرض کو ان کے حصے دیے جاتے ہیں۔ اگر چھٹا حصہ بچ جائے تو وہ دادا کو مل جاتا ہے۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1076" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1076</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1076 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik se riwayat ki ke unhone suna ke Sulayman bin Yasar ne kaha: Umar bin al-Khattab, Uthman bin Affan, aur Zayd bin Thabit (Radi Allahu Anhum) ne dada ko sagay behan bhaiyon ke sath ek tihai hissa diya. Imam Malik ne kaha: Hamare haan jo tariqa muttafiqa hai aur jo main ne hamare shehar ke ahl-e-ilm ko karte dekha hai woh yeh hai ke dada baap ki maujoodgi mein bilkul waris nahin hota. Usay bete aur pote ke sath chatha hissa batour-e-farz diya jata hai. Is ke ilawa, jab mayyat ki maa ya phuphi na ho, to pehle ashab-e-farz ko un ke hissay diye jate hain. Agar chatha hissa bach jaye to woh dada ko mil jata hai.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1076" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 1068

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1077]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1077" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1077</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1077 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ عُثْمَانَ بْنِ إِسْحَاقَ بْنِ خَرَشَةَ، عَنْ قَبِيصَةَ بْنِ ذُؤَيْبٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ جَاءَتِ الْجَدَّةُ إِلَى أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ تَسْأَلُهُ مِيرَاثَهَا فَقَالَ لَهَا أَبُو بَكْرٍ مَا لَكِ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ شَىْءٌ وَمَا عَلِمْتُ لَكِ فِي سُنَّةِ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم شَيْئًا فَارْجِعِي حَتَّى أَسْأَلَ النَّاسَ فَسَأَلَ النَّاسَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ الْمُغِيرَةُ بْنُ شُعْبَةَ حَضَرْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَعْطَاهَا السُّدُسَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ هَلْ مَعَكَ غَيْرُكَ فَقَامَ مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ مَسْلَمَةَ الأَنْصَارِيُّ فَقَالَ مِثْلَ مَا قَالَ الْمُغِيرَةُ فَأَنْفَذَهُ لَهَا أَبُو بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقُ ثُمَّ جَاءَتِ الْجَدَّةُ الأُخْرَى إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ تَسْأَلُهُ مِيرَاثَهَا فَقَالَ لَهَا مَا لَكِ فِي كِتَابِ اللَّهِ شَىْءٌ وَمَا كَانَ الْقَضَاءُ الَّذِي قُضِيَ بِهِ إِلاَّ لِغَيْرِكِ وَمَا أَنَا بِزَائِدٍ فِي الْفَرَائِضِ شَيْئًا وَلَكِنَّهُ ذَلِكَ السُّدُسُ فَإِنِ اجْتَمَعْتُمَا فَهُوَ بَيْنَكُمَا وَأَيَّتُكُمَا خَلَتْ بِهِ فَهُوَ لَهَا ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1077" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: Muhammad, as explicitly attributed in this complete source field. Exact English field independently read in full. This locally supported Prophetic report is preserved as its own exact source passage; companion/jurist discussion outside the selected passage remains source context. No authenticity or translation-equivalence judgment is made.</p>

<p dir="ltr">al-Mughira ibn Shuba said, &#x27;I was present with the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, when he gave the grandmother a sixth.&#x27; Abu Bakr said, &#x27;Was there anybody else with you?&#x27; Muhammad ibn Maslama al-Ansari stood up and said the like of what al-Mughira said.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1077]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1077" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1077</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1077 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>English · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Uthman ibn Ishaq ibn Kharasha that Qabisa ibn Dhu&#x27;ayb said, &quot;A grandmother came to Abu Bakr as-Siddiq and asked him for her inheritance. Abu Bakr said to her, &#x27;You have nothing in the Book of Allah, and I do not know that you have anything in the sunna of the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace. Go away therefore, until I have questioned the people.&#x27; (i.e.the Companions). He questioned the people, and </p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1077" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="ltr"> Abu Bakr as-Siddiq gave it to her. Then the other grandmother came to Umar ibn al-Khattab and asked him for her inheritance. He said to her, &quot;You have nothing in the Book of Allah, and what has been decided is only for other than you, and I am not one to add to the fixed shares, other than that sixth. If there are two of you together, it is between you. If eitherof you is left alone with it, it is hers</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1077" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Hindi</h3>

<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1077</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1077 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">क़ाबिसा बिन जूवेइब से रिवायत है के मय्यत की नानी अबू बक्र सिद्दीक़ (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास मीरास मांगने आयी. अबू बक्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा अल्लाह की किताब में तेरा कुछ हिस्सा मुक़र्रर नहीं है और न मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से इस बाब में कोई हदीथ सुनी है तो वापस जा. मैंने लोगों से पुछा तो मुग़ीरा बिन शुबा ने कहा के मैं इस वक़्त मौजूद था मेरे सामने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने नानी को छठा हिस्सा (१/६) दिलाया है. अबू बक्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा कोई और भी तुम्हारे साथ है? तो मुहम्मद बिन मसलमा अंसारी खड़े हुए और जैसा मुग़ीरा बिन शुबा ने कहा था वैसा ही बयां किया. अबू बकर ने छटा हिस्सा उसको दिला दिया. फिर हज़रात उम्र (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के वक़्त में एक दादी मीरास मांगने आयी तेरा कुछ हिस्सा मज़कूर नहीं और पहले जो हुकुम हो चूका है वह नानी के बारे में हुआ था और मैं अपनी तरफ से फ़राइज़ में कुछ बाधा नहीं सकता लेकिन वही छटा हिस्सा तू भी ले तुम दोनों में से एक अकेली हो वही छटा हिस्सा ले ले.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1077" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قبیصہ بن ذویب سے روایت ہے کہ میت کی نانی ابوبکر صدیق رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس میراث مانگنے آئی ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا اللہ کی کتاب میں تیرا کچھ حصہ مقرر نہیں ہے اور نہ میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم سے اس باب میں کوئی حدیث سنی ہے تو واپس جا۔ میں نے لوگوں سے پوچھا تو مغیرہ بن شعبہ نے کہا کہ میں اس وقت موجود تھا میرے سامنے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وآلہ وسلم نے نانی کو چھٹا حصہ دلایا ہے ابوبکر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا کوئی اور بھی تمہارے ساتھ ہے تو محمد بن مسلمہ انصاری کھڑے ہوئے اور جیسا مغیرہ بن شعبہ نے کہا تھا ویسا ہی بیان کیا ابوبکر نے چھٹا حصہ اس کو دلا دیا پھر حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے وقت میں ایک دادی میراث مانگنے آئی حضرت عمر رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے کہا اللہ کی کتاب میں تیرا کچھ حصہ مذکور نہیں اور پہلے جو حکم ہو چکا ہے وہ نانی کے بارے میں ہوا تھا اور میں اپنی طرف سے فرائض میں کچھ بڑھا نہیں سکتا لیکن وہی چھٹا حصہ تو بھی لے اگر نانی بھی ہو تو دونوں سدس کا بانٹ لو اور جو تم دونوں میں سے ایک اکیلی ہو وہی چھٹا حصہ لے لے ۔</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1077" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1077]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1077" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1077</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1077 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Qabisa bin Zuweib se riwayat hai ke mayyat ki nani Abu Bakr Siddiq (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas meeras mangne aayi. Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha Allah ki kitab mein tera kuch hissa muqarrar nahi hai aur na maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se is baab mein koi hadith suni hai to wapas ja. Maine logon se poocha to Mughira bin Shuba ne kaha ke main is waqt maujood tha mere samne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne nani ko chhata hissa (1/6) dilaya hai. Abu Bakr (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha koi aur bhi tumhare sath hai? To Muhammad bin Maslama Ansari khade hue aur jaisa Mughira bin Shuba ne kaha tha waisa hi bayan kiya. Abu Bakr ne chhata hissa usko dila diya. Phir Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke waqt mein ek dadi meeras mangne aayi, Hazrat Umar (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha Allah ki kitab mein tera kuch hissa mazkoor nahi aur pehle jo hukum ho chuka hai woh nani ke bare mein hua tha aur main apni taraf se faraiz mein kuch badha nahi sakta lekin wahi chhata hissa tu bhi le, agar nani bhi ho to dono sudus (1/6) ka baant lo aur jo tum dono mein se ek akeli ho wahi chhata hissa le le.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1077" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ يَحْيَى بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، عَنِ الْقَاسِمِ بْنِ مُحَمَّدٍ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ أَتَتِ الْجَدَّتَانِ إِلَى أَبِي بَكْرٍ الصِّدِّيقِ فَأَرَادَ أَنْ يَجْعَلَ السُّدُسَ، لِلَّتِي مِنْ قِبَلِ الأُمِّ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَجُلٌ مِنَ الأَنْصَارِ أَمَا إِنَّكَ تَتْرُكُ الَّتِي لَوْ مَاتَتْ وَهُوَ حَىٌّ كَانَ إِيَّاهَا يَرِثُ فَجَعَلَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ السُّدُسَ بَيْنَهُمَا ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abu Bakr asSiddiq (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Yahya ibn Said that al-Qasim ibn Muhammad said, &quot;Two grandmothers came to Abu Bakr asSiddiq, and he wanted to give the sixth to the one who was from the mother&#x27;s side, and a man of the Ansar said, &#x27;What? Are you omitting the one from whom he would inherit if she died while he was alive?&#x27; Abu Bakr divided the sixth between them</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1078]</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">क़ासिम बिन मुहम्मद से रिवायत है के नानी और दादी दोनों आयी हज़रात अबू बक्र सिद्दीक़ (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के पास. अबू बक्र सिद्दीक़ (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने सुदूस यानि छटा हिस्सा नानी को देना चाहा. एक शख्स अंसारी बोलै ए अबू बक्र तुम उसको नहीं दिलाते जो अगर मर जाती और मय्यत ज़िंदा होता यानि पोता और वरिस होता मय्यत ज़िंदा होता यानि उसका नवसा तो वारिस न होता. फिर अबू बक्र सिद्दीक़ (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने ये सुन कर सुदूस (१/६) उन दोनों को दिलाया.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1078" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">قاسم بن محمد سے روایت ہے کہ نانی اور دادی دونوں آئیں حضرت ابوبکر صدیق رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ کے پاس، ابوبکر صدیق نے سدس یعنی چھٹا حصہ نانی کو دینا چاہا ایک شخص انصاری بولا اے ابوبکر تم اس کو نہیں دلاتے جو اگر مرجاتی اور میت زندہ ہوتا یعنی پوتا اور وارث ہوتا اور اس کو دلاتے ہو جو اگر مرجاتی اور میت زندہ ہوتا یعنی اس کا نواسہ تو وارث نہ ہوتا پھر ابوبکر صدیق رضی اللہ تعالیٰ عنہ نے یہ سن کر سدس ان دونوں کو دلایا۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Qasim bin Muhammad se riwayat hai ke nani aur dadi dono aayi Hazrat Abu Bakr Siddiq (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke paas. Abu Bakr Siddiq (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne sudus yani chhata hissa nani ko dena chaha. Ek shakhs Ansari bola aye Abu Bakr tum usko nahi dilate jo agar mar jati aur mayyat zinda hota yani pota aur waris hota, aur usko dilate ho jo agar mar jati aur mayyat zinda hota yani uska nawasa to waris na hota. Phir Abu Bakr Siddiq (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne ye sun kar sudus (1/6) un dono ko dilaya.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1078" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

## Narration 1070

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1079)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 27</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ عَبْدِ رَبِّهِ بْنِ سَعِيدٍ، أَنَّ أَبَا بَكْرِ بْنَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ الْحَارِثِ بْنِ هِشَامٍ، كَانَ لاَ يَفْرِضُ إِلاَّ لِلْجَدَّتَيْنِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ الأَمْرُ الْمُجْتَمَعُ عَلَيْهِ عِنْدَنَا الَّذِي لاَ اخْتِلاَفَ فِيهِ وَالَّذِي أَدْرَكْتُ عَلَيْهِ أَهْلَ الْعِلْمِ بِبَلَدِنَا أَنَّ الْجَدَّةَ أُمَّ الأُمِّ لاَ تَرِثُ مَعَ الأُمِّ دِنْيَا شَيْئًا وَهِيَ فِيمَا سِوَى ذَلِكَ يُفْرَضُ لَهَا السُّدُسُ فَرِيضَةً وَأَنَّ الْجَدَّةَ أُمَّ الأَبِ لاَ تَرِثُ مَعَ الأُمِّ وَلاَ مَعَ الأَبِ شَيْئًا وَهِيَ فِيمَا سِوَى ذَلِكَ يُفْرَضُ لَهَا السُّدُسُ فَرِيضَةً فَإِذَا اجْتَمَعَتِ الْجَدَّتَانِ أُمُّ الأَبِ وَأُمُّ الأُمِّ وَلَيْسَ لِلْمُتَوَفَّى دُونَهُمَا أَبٌ وَلاَ أُمٌّ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ فَإِنِّي سَمِعْتُ أَنَّ أُمَّ الأُمِّ إِنْ كَانَتْ أَقْعَدَهُمَا كَانَ لَهَا السَّدُسُ دُونَ أُمِّ الأَبِ وَإِنْ كَانَتْ أُمُّ الأَبِ أَقْعَدَهُمَا أَوْ كَانَتَا فِي الْقُعْدَدِ مِنَ الْمُتَوَفَّى بِمَنْزِلَةٍ سَوَاءً فَإِنَّ السُّدُسَ بَيْنَهُمَا نِصْفَانِ ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ وَلاَ مِيرَاثَ لأَحَدٍ مِنَ الْجَدَّاتِ إِلاَّ لِلْجَدَّتَيْنِ لأَنَّهُ بَلَغَنِي أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَرَّثَ الْجَدَّةَ ثُمَّ سَأَلَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ عَنْ ذَلِكَ حَتَّى أَتَاهُ الثَّبَتُ عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهُ وَرَّثَ الْجَدَّةَ فَأَنْفَذَهُ لَهَا ثُمَّ أَتَتِ الْجَدَّةُ الأُخْرَى إِلَى عُمَرَ بْنِ الْخَطَّابِ فَقَالَ لَهَا مَا أَنَا بِزَائِدٍ فِي الْفَرَائِضِ شَيْئًا فَإِنِ اجْتَمَعْتُمَا فَهُوَ بَيْنَكُمَا وَأَيَّتُكُمَا خَلَتْ بِهِ فَهُوَ لَهَا ‏.‏ قَالَ مَالِكٌ ثُمَّ لَمْ نَعْلَمْ أَحَدًا وَرَّثَ غَيْرَ جَدَّتَيْنِ مُنْذُ كَانَ الإِسْلاَمُ إِلَى الْيَوْمِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1079]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1079" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Abdu Rabbih ibn Said that Abu Bakr ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Harith ibn Hisham only gave a fixed share to two grandmothers (together). Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us in which there is no dispute and which I saw the people of knowledge in our city doing, is that the maternal grandmother does not inherit anything at all with the mother. Outside of that, she is given a sixth as a fixed share. The paternal grandmotherdoes not inherit anything along with the mother or the father. Outside of that she is given a sixth as a fixed share.&quot; If both the paternal grandmother and maternal grandmother are alive, and the deceased does not have a father or mother outside of them, Malik said,.&quot;I have heard that if the maternal grandmother is the nearest of the two of them, then she has a sixth instead of the paternal grandmother. If the paternal grandmother is nearer, or they are in the same position in relation to the deceased, the sixth is divided equally between them.&quot; Malik said, &quot;None of the female grand-relations except for these two has any inheritance because I have heard that the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, gave the grandmother inheritance, and then Abu Bakr asked about that until someone reliable related from the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, that he had made the grandmother an heir and given a share to her. Another grandmother came to Umar ibn al-Khattab, and he said, &#x27;I am not one to add to fixed shares. If there are two of you together, it is between you. If either of you is left alone with it, it is hers.&#x27; &quot; Malik said, &quot;We do not know of anyone who made other than the two grandmothers heirs from the beginning of Islam to this day</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1079]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1079" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">याह्या ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक रिवायत करते हैं के उन्होंने सिर्फ दो दादियों को इकठा एक हिस्सा दिया. इमाम मलिक ने कहा: हमारे नज़दीक मुत्तफ़िक़ा तरीक़ा जिस में कोई इख्तिलाफ नहीं और जिसे मैं ने हमारे शहर के अहल-इ-इल्म को करते देखा है वह यह है के नञि माँ की मौजूदगी में वारिस नहीं होती. इस के इलावा उसे छठा हिस्सा बतौर-इ-फ़र्ज़ दिया जाता है. दादी माँ या बाप की मौजूदगी में वारिस नहीं होती. इस के इलावा उसे छठा हिस्सा बतौर-इ-फ़र्ज़ दिया जाता है.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1079]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1079" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یحییٰ نے مجھ سے امام مالک، عبد ربہ بن سعید سے اور وہ ابوبکر بن عبد الرحمٰن بن الحارث بن ہشام سے روایت کرتے ہیں کہ انہوں نے صرف دو دادیوں کو اکٹھا ایک حصہ دیا۔ امام مالک نے کہا: ہمارے نزدیک متفقہ طریقہ جس میں کوئی اختلاف نہیں اور جسے میں نے ہمارے شہر کے اہل علم کو کرتے دیکھا ہے وہ یہ ہے کہ نانی ماں کی موجودگی میں وارث نہیں ہوتی۔ اس کے علاوہ اسے چھٹا حصہ بطور فرض دیا جاتا ہے۔ دادی ماں یا باپ کی موجودگی میں وارث نہیں ہوتی۔ اس کے علاوہ اسے چھٹا حصہ بطور فرض دیا جاتا ہے۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1079]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1079" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya ne mujh se Imam Malik, Abdu Rabbih ibn Said se aur woh Abu Bakr ibn Abd ar-Rahman ibn al-Harith ibn Hisham se riwayat karte hain ke unhone sirf do dadiyon ko ikatha ek hissa diya. Imam Malik ne kaha: Hamare nazdeek muttafiqa tariqa jis mein koi ikhtilaf nahin aur jisay main ne hamare shehar ke ahl-e-ilm ko karte dekha hai woh yeh hai ke nani maa ki maujoodgi mein waris nahin hoti. Is ke ilawa usay chatha hissa batour-e-farz diya jata hai. Dadi maa ya baap ki maujoodgi mein waris nahin hoti. Is ke ilawa usay chatha hissa batour-e-farz diya jata hai.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1079]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1079" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 1071

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A1080)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 27</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ زَيْدِ بْنِ أَسْلَمَ، أَنَّ عُمَرَ بْنَ الْخَطَّابِ، سَأَلَ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنِ الْكَلاَلَةِ فَقَالَ لَهُ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ يَكْفِيكَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ الآيَةُ الَّتِي أُنْزِلَتْ فِي الصَّيْفِ آخِرَ سُورَةِ النِّسَاءِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 1080]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1080" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 1080</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Muwatta Malik   1080 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Zayd ibn Aslam that Umar ibn al-Khattab asked the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, about someone who died without parents or offspring, and the Messenger of Allah, may Allah bless him and grant him peace, said to him, &quot;The ayat which was sent down in the summer at the end of the Surat an-Nisa (Sura 4) is enoughfor you.&quot; Malik said, &quot;The generally agreed on way of doing things among us, in which there is no dispute, and which I saw the people of knowledge in our city doing, is that the person who leaves neither parent or offspring can be of two types. As for the kind described in the ayat which was sent down at the beginning of the Surat an-Nisa in which Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted! said, &#x27;If a man or a woman has no direct heir, but has a brother or a sister by the mother, each of the two has a sixth. If there are more than that, they share equally in a third.&#x27; (Sura 4 ayat 12) This heirless one does not have heirs among his mother&#x27;s siblings since there are no children or parents. As for the other kind described in the ayat which comes at the end of the Surat an-Nisa, Allah, the Blessed, the Exalted, said in it, &#x27;They will ask you for a decision. Say, &quot;Allah gives you a decision about the indirect heirs. If a man perishes having no children, but he has a sister, she shall receive a half of what he leaves, and he is her heir if she has no children. If there are two sisters, they shall receive two-thirds of what he leaves. If there are brothers and sisters, the male shall receive the portion of two females. Allah makes clear to you that you might not go astray. Allah has knowledge of everything&quot; &#x27; &quot; (Sura 4 ayat 176). Malik said, &quot;If this person without direct heirs (parents) or children has siblings by the father, they inherit with the grandfather from the person without direct heirs. The grandfather inherits with the siblings because he is more entitled to the inheritance than them. That is because he inherits a sixth with the male children of the deceased when the siblings do not inherit anything with the male children of the deceased. How can he not be like one of them when he takes a sixth with the children of the deceased? How can he not take a third with the siblings while the brother&#x27;s sons take a third with them? The grandfather is the one who overshadows the half-siblings by the mother and keeps them from inheriting. He is more entitled to what they have because they are omitted for his sake. If the grandfather did not take that third, the half-siblings by the mother would take it and would take what does not return to the half-siblings by the father. The half-siblings by the mother are more entitled to that third than the half-siblings by the father while the grandfather is more entitled to that than the half- siblings by the mother</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 1080]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1080" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यह्या ने मुझ से इमाम मालिक से, उन्होंने ज़ैद बिन असलम से रिवायत किया के हज़रत उमर बिन खत्ताब (रज़ी अल्लाह-ओ-अन्हु) ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से उस शख़्स के बारे में पूछा जिस का इंतिक़ाल हो जाए और उस के वालिदैन और औलाद न हों, तो रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया के सूरह अन-निसा के आख़िर में नाज़िल होने वाली आयत तुम्हारे लिए काफ़ी है। इमाम मालिक (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) ने फ़रमाया के हमारे यहाँ जो तरीक़ा मुत्तफ़िक़ अलैह है जिस में कोई इख़्तिलाफ़ नहीं और मैं ने अपने शहर के अह्ल-ए-इल्म को इस पर अमल करते देखा है, वो ये है के जिस शख़्स के न वालिदैन हों और न औलाद, उस की दो क़िस्में हैं। एक वो जिस का ज़िक्र सूरह अन-निसा के शुरू में है के अल्लाह तआला ने फ़रमाया अगर किसी मर्द या औरत के बराह-ए-रास्त वारिस न हों मगर माँ की तरफ़ से भाई या बहन हो, तो दोनों में से हर एक के लिए छठा हिस्सा है, अगर वो इस से ज़्यादा हों तो वो एक तिहाई में बराबर के शरीक होंगे। ये वारिस माँ की तरफ़ से रिश्तेदारों में से नहीं होता क्योंकि औलाद और वालिदैन मौजूद नहीं हैं। दूसरी क़िसम वो है जिस का ज़िक्र सूरह अन-निसा के आख़िर में है जिस में अल्लाह तआला ने फ़रमाया के लोग आप से फ़तवा पूछते हैं... (सूरह अन-निसा आयत 176)। इमाम मालिक (रहमतुल्लाह अलैह) ने फ़रमाया के अगर इस कलालह के बाप की तरफ़ से भाई बहन हों तो वो दादा के साथ मीरास पाएंगे क्योंकि दादा बहन भाइयों के मुक़ाबले में मीरास का ज़्यादा हक़दार है...</p>

<p dir="auto">[Muwatta Malik · 1080]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/1080" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
